Method: 基于引导势梯度与荷守恒两大公设，通过非相对论极限验证牛顿引力与库仑定律，再由相对论协变性将引导势推广至时空度规与规范矢量场；利用紧致李群性质与手征费米子反常相消约束可能的规范群，并施加最小秩与表示内容要求进行筛选。

Result: 严格证明内对称群必须是紧致李群；在反常相消与最小性条件下，标准模型规范群SU(3)_c × SU(2)_L × U(1)_Y被唯一选出；给出强、弱相互作用的引导场描述；预言等效原理破缺等可观测效应；提出黑洞物理中引导视角下的色禁闭类比与奇点回避机制。

Conclusion: 该框架为标准模型的规范结构提供了坚实的第一性原理基础，不仅统一了基本相互作用的几何与动力学起源，还衍生出可检验的新物理预言，尤其在等效原理检验与引力波环降信号方面具有潜在观测意义。

Abstract: We construct a unified theory of interactions from two fundamental postulates: (1) all elementary forces arise as gradients of guidance potentials, and (2) the charges that generate these potentials are conserved. In the non-relativistic limit, a scalar guidance field reproduces Newtonian gravity and Coulomb's law. Relativistic consistency forces the guidance potentials to be upgraded to a spacetime metric and gauge vector fields. The central advance of this paper is the rigorous derivation that the internal symmetry group must be a compact Lie group; combined with anomaly cancellation for chiral fermions, this compactness severely constrains the admissible gauge groups. Under the additional requirement of minimal rank and representation content, the framework uniquely singles out the Standard Model's $SU(3)_c \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_Y$ as the most economical choice among all compact anomaly-free groups. The framework thus provides a first-principles understanding of the gauge structure of the Standard Model. We also give a guidance-field description of strong and weak interactions, and discuss testable signals such as violations of the equivalence principle arising from nonlinear superposition of guidance potentials. As an application, we explore the implications for black hole physics, where the guidance perspective suggests a duality with color confinement and a natural mechanism for singularity avoidance, with possible observational signatures in the gravitational-wave ringdown.

</details>


### [102] [Singly heavy tetraquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25401)
*Jun-Jie Liu,Zhi-Biao Liang,Feng-Xiao Liu,Mu-Yang Chen,Xian-Hui Zhong,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了单重味四夸克态的1S波谱，采用半相对论混合夸克势模型（包含单胶子交换和单介子交换相互作用），并结合夸克交换模型计算其碎裂衰变。结果表明OBE势对谱结构至关重要；所有态均为紧致态，衰变宽度较窄（1–120 MeV）；部分实验共振态（如Ds0(2317)等）无法解释为紧致四夸克态，而Tc̄s0(2900)和Tc̄s0(2870)则可能对应特定量子数的四夸克态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解实验上观测到的疑似四夸克态共振峰是否可由紧致四夸克图像解释，并厘清不同相互作用（OGE与OBE）在谱学中的相对贡献。

Method: 采用半相对论混合夸克势模型计算单重味四夸克1S波能谱，同时引入夸克交换模型计算其碎裂衰变宽度。

Result: OBE势对能谱起关键作用；所有计算态均为紧致态且衰变宽度窄（1–120 MeV）；Ds0(2317)、Ds1(2460)、Tb̄s(5568)、Tc̄s(2327)不能被解释为紧致四夸克；Tc̄s0(2870)和Tc̄s0(2900)分别支持IJ^P=00^+和10^+的四夸克态解释；多个新四夸克态有望在实验中通过主导衰变道被发现。

Conclusion: 单重味四夸克态普遍存在且多为紧致结构，OBE效应不可忽略；部分实验共振态更可能为分子态或其它结构，而新发现的Tc̄s0态则为紧致四夸克的有力候选者；后续实验应在预测的主导衰变道中进一步搜寻更多此类态。

Abstract: In this work, we carry out a systematic study of the spectra of the $1S$-wave states for the whole singly-heavy tetraquark systems within a semi-relativistic hybrid quark potential model, in which both the one-gluon exchange (OGE) and one-boson exchange (OBE) interactions are included. Furthermore, the fall-apart decays are evaluated with the quark exchange model by combining the obtained spectra. It is found that besides the OGE potentials, the OBE potentials play crucial roles for describing the spectrum. All of our obtained states lie far above the lowest dissociation meson-meson threshold. They are compact states with relatively narrow fall-apart widths $\sim 1-120$~MeV. The $D_{s0}(2317)$, $D_{s1}(2460)$, $T_{b\bar{s}}(5568)$, and $T_{c\bar{s}}(2327)$ resonances reported from experiments cannot be explained as compact tetraquarks. While the $T_{\bar{c}\bar{s}0}(2870)$ and $T_{c\bar{s}0}(2900)$ favor the tetraquark states with $IJ^P=00^+$ and $10^+$, i.e. $T_{(\bar{c}\bar{s}[ud])0^+}^0(2919)$ and $T_{(cn\{\bar{s}\bar{n}\})0^+}^{1}(2922)$, respectively. More singly-heavy tetraquark states have good potentials to be observed in some of their dominant decay channels in experiments.

</details>


### [103] [Distinguishing the Axion-Wind and Oscillating-EDM Couplings in Storage-Ring Searches for Axion Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25466)
*V. Hejny,J. Pretz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种方法来区分存储环中轴子和类轴子粒子暗物质的两种耦合效应：振荡电偶极矩（EDM）和轴子风耦合，二者在标准观测量上简并；通过同时测量垂直极化建立和自旋进动相位漂移，或调节电/磁弯曲场比值，可分离这两种效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子和类轴子粒子暗物质的两种耦合（EDM与轴子风）在存储环共振自旋进动实验中产生相同频率的垂直极化信号，导致简并，需新方法加以区分。

Method: 1）在固定能量多束团配置下同时测量垂直极化建立与自旋进动相位漂移；2）在联合存储环中调节电/磁弯曲场比值而保持共振频率不变。

Result: 两种方法均可实现对EDM与轴子风耦合强度的独立提取，前者存在二重符号简并，后者可干净分离两种效应。

Conclusion: 所提方法为未来专用存储环EDM实验提供了可行路径，以明确识别轴子类暗物质的不同耦合机制。

Abstract: Resonant spin-precession experiments in storage rings are sensitive to two distinct axion and axion-like particle dark-matter couplings: an oscillating electric dipole moment (EDM) primarily driven by the axion-gluon interaction, and the axion-wind (derivative) coupling acting as an effective pseudomagnetic field on the spin. Both effects produce a vertical polarization buildup at the same resonance frequency and are therefore degenerate in this standard observable. It is shown that the two couplings enter the vertical build-up and the spin-precession phase walk as the difference and sum of their resonance strengths, respectively. Measuring both observables simultaneously at fixed energy in a multi-bunch configuration allows the individual coupling strengths to be extracted up to a two-fold sign ambiguity. As a complementary approach, the ratio of electric to magnetic bending fields in a combined storage ring can be varied while keeping the resonance frequency fixed. This leaves the axion-wind contribution unchanged while modifying the EDM contribution in a calculable way, enabling a clean separation of the two effects. Both approaches can be used in future storage-ring facilities dedicated to EDM measurements.

</details>


### [104] [Photon Gas Thermodynamics in Doubly Special Relativity at the Planck Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25473)
*Qi Xiong,Xinyi Yang,Guifeng Su,Yi Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Magueijo-Smolin双特殊相对论框架下光子气体的热力学性质，推导出其对数巨配分函数及全部热力学量，并发现当温度接近普朗克尺度时，所有热力学函数均被系统性抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索双特殊相对论（含普朗克能标作为不变能量尺度）对光子气体热力学行为的影响，以检验该理论在极端条件下的物理效应。

Method: 基于Magueijo-Smolin双特殊相对论模型，推导光子气体的对数巨配分函数，并由此计算Helmholtz自由能、内能、熵、压强和热容等全套热力学量，辅以数值分析。

Result: 得到所有热力学量的显式表达式；当不变能量尺度趋于无穷时，结果退化为标准狭义相对论形式；在普朗克温度附近，各热力学量均被有限截断所抑制。

Conclusion: 双特殊相对论在普朗克能标附近显著修正光子气体热力学行为，表明该框架可能对早期宇宙或高能物理过程提供可观测效应。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamics of a photon gas within the Magueijo-Smolin formulation of doubly special relativity, a framework that augments the speed of light with an observer-independent energy scale of the order of the Planck energy. We derive the logarithmic grand partition function, and the complete set of thermodynamic quantities for the photon gas, including the Helmholtz free energy, internal energy, entropy, pressure, and heat capacity, and perform their numerical evaluation. Our results smoothly reduce to the conventional special relativistic expressions in the limit where the invariant energy scale tends to infinity. For temperatures approaching the Planck scale, the finite cutoff induces a systematic suppression of all thermodynamic functions relative to their standard special-relativistic counterparts.

</details>


### [105] [Pseudoscalar Yukawa Coupling Induced Helical Asymmetry in Fermionic Preheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25521)
*Ke FU,Li-Shuang Liu,Yu-Feng Wang,Xin-Yu Gu,Xi-Bin Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀后预热阶段中费米子的螺旋不对称性，通过引入一个伪标量Yukawa耦合模型，分析了狄拉克场与暴胀子之间的相互作用，并利用WKB方法解析计算了Bogoliubov系数的跃迁关系，揭示了螺旋不对称粒子产生的机制及其在膨胀宇宙中的分布特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀后预热阶段中费米子螺旋不对称性的起源及其对早期宇宙物理过程的影响。

Method: 构建伪标量Yukawa耦合模型，结合WKB近似方法解析计算Bogoliubov系数跃迁，并辅以数值模拟验证。

Result: 发现伪质量m5变号导致螺旋不对称费米子产生；虚部相位Θ^{(r)}_{1}引起高斯分布偏离，实部则显著增强各螺旋态数密度；结果得到数值模拟支持。

Conclusion: 预热阶段可通过伪标量耦合机制自然产生螺旋不对称费米子，该机制受非绝热演化、Pauli阻塞与参量共振共同调控，具有可观测的理论意义。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate the fermionic helical asymmetry during preheating by introducing a model in which the Dirac field $ψ$ couples to the scalar inflaton $φ$ directly through the pseudoscalar Yukawa mechanism described by the term $- g\fracφ{f} m_ψ\barψ\mathrm{i}γ^5 ψ$. Following inflation, as the inflaton oscillates around the minimum of its potential, the effective frequency of Dirac field changes non-adiabatically, resulting in particle production. These oscillations also indicate that the pseudo-mass $m_5 = g φm_ψ/f$ changes the sign, leading to the production of helical-asymmetric fermions. We employ the WKB method to analytically calculate the transition relations of the Bogoliubov coefficients, which is valid for an arbitrary number of productions and demonstrates a significant difference compared to the case without asymmetry. In the expanding universe, the presence of pseudo-mass $m_5$ alters the Gaussian distributions for each helical state, since the combined effects of Pauli blocking and parametric resonance produce coherent enhancement and coherent suppression in certain frequency ranges. This departure arises from the imaginary component of the first-order adiabatic complex phase, denoted as $Θ^{(r)}_{1}$. The real part of $Θ^{(r)}_{1}$ significantly enhances the number density of each helical state after eight oscillations of the inflaton since the coherent superposition. These conclusions have also been validated by the numerical results.

</details>


### [106] [Virtual state and two-pole structure: $Ξ(1690)$ and $Ξ(1820)$ from BESIII data on $ψ(3686)\to K^-Λ\barΞ^+$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25606)
*Jun He*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过耦合通道重散射框架分析BESIII实验数据，发现Ξ(1690)更可能是一个位于K̄Σ阈值下方的虚态，而Ξ(1820)则呈现由KΣ*和πΞ*通道主导的双极点结构，类似于Λ(1405)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Ξ(1690)和Ξ(1820)共振态的本质，特别是其是否为动力学生成的强子分子态或夸克态。

Method: 采用耦合通道重散射模型，包含K̄Λ、K̄Σ(*)、πΞ(*)、ηΞ(*)和KΩ等通道，直接拟合ψ(3686)→K⁻ΛΞ̄⁺实验数据。

Result: Ξ(1690)被确定为K̄Σ阈值附近的虚态，解释其在K⁻Λ不变质量谱中的尖锐峰；Ξ(1820)显示双极点结构，源自KΣ*与πΞ*通道耦合，解释其异常宽的宽度。

Conclusion: Ξ(1690)和Ξ(1820)均为动力学生成的共振态，而非传统夸克模型预期的三夸克态，凸显高精度粲偶素衰变数据对理解超子共振态的重要性。

Abstract: We investigate the nature of the $Ξ(1690)$ and $Ξ(1820)$ resonances by analyzing the recent BESIII data on $ψ(3686) \to K^-Λ\barΞ^+$ within a coupled-channel rescattering framework. The calculation includes the channels $\bar{K}Λ$, $\bar{K}Σ^{(*)}$, $πΞ^{(*)}$, $ηΞ^{(*)}$, and $KΩ$. A direct fit to the data reveals that the $Ξ(1690)$ is best described as a virtual state located right below the $\bar{K}Σ$ threshold, a feature that naturally explains its very sharp peak observed in the $K^-Λ$ invariant mass spectrum. In contrast, the $Ξ(1820)$ exhibits a two-pole structure originating mainly from the coupled contributions of the $KΣ^*$ and $πΞ^*$ channels, showing a two-pole pattern similar to that of the well-known $Λ(1405)$ and accounting for the unexpectedly broad width measured by BESIII. These findings provide new insights into the dynamical generation of hyperon resonances and highlight the importance of high-precision data from charmonium decays.

</details>


### [107] [Constraining axion quadratic couplings with the Hulse-Taylor binary system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25631)
*Ziwen Yin,Shyam Balaji,Malcolm Fairbairn,David J. E. Marsh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用赫尔斯-泰勒双中子星系统的高精度轨道演化数据，对轴子与中子、电子和缪子的耦合强度施加了新的限制，特别是对轴子-缪子二次耦合给出了目前最强的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用双中子星系统作为高精度实验室，检验广义相对论并探索超出标准模型的新物理（如轴子）存在的可能性。

Method: 基于广义相对论对Hulse-Taylor双中子星轨道衰减的精确描述，分析轴子-费米子二次耦合导致的偶极与四极轴子辐射及长程力效应，并结合观测数据推导耦合参数约束。

Result: 对轻QCD轴子，得到轴子衰变常数f_a的新限制，与其它探针互补；首次给出轴子-缪子二次耦合的最强实验限制。

Conclusion: 双中子星系统是探测极轻轴子及其与重费米子耦合的有效天体物理实验室，可补充地面与天体物理其他观测手段。

Abstract: The orbital evolution of the Hulse-Taylor binary neutron star system is described to high precision by general relativity, in which gravity is the only long-range force and gravitational waves provide the dominant energy-loss channel. We use this precision test of relativistic binary dynamics to derive new constraints on axion couplings to stable neutron star constituents: neutrons, electrons, and muons. Quadratic shift symmetry breaking axion-fermion couplings allow binary systems to lose energy through dipole and quadrupole emission of axion waves. These couplings also mediate long range, spin independent forces in two different regimes: in an ambient DM background, and when a tachyonic phase transition is triggered inside the neutron stars. For light QCD axions our constraints can be recast as limits on the axion decay constant, which are complementary to other probes for $m_a\lesssim 10^{-12}\text{ eV}$ and $f_a\lesssim M_\textrm{pl}$. We also place the strongest constraint available on the axion-muon quadratic coupling, which is otherwise only constrained by supernova cooling.

</details>


### [108] [The 95 GeV Excess in Models with Two Higgs Doublets plus One Singlet: Model Distinction via Four-top Final States and Triple Higgs Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25670)
*S. Heinemeyer,C. Li,G. Moortgat-Pick,D. Schieber*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在实验上区分N2HDM和2HDMS两种扩展希格斯模型，通过分析其标量势和CP-奇数扇区可观测量，特别是三线性希格斯耦合及四顶夸克、双希格斯产生过程，在HL-LHC和未来正负电子对撞机上实现模型区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N2HDM和2HDMS模型均能解释约95 GeV的希格斯信号超出，且预测相似，需寻找更精细的可观测量来区分二者。

Method: 推导三线性希格斯耦合解析表达式，分析HL-LHC上的四顶夸克产生（探针赝标量扇区）和未来e+e−对撞机上的双希格斯产生（直接探针三线性耦合），并定义2σ可区分所需的最小偏离。

Result: 发现两类模型在四顶夸克与双希格斯产生截面存在可观测差异，可在HL-LHC和未来e+e−对撞机上实现2σ水平区分；该方法具有一般性，适用于其他单态扩展希格斯模型判别。

Conclusion: 尽管N2HDM与2HDMS在低能希格斯现象中高度简并，但通过高阶过程（如四顶夸克、双希格斯产生）结合标量势结构分析，可在未来对撞机上有效区分二者，为扩展希格斯扇区提供关键判据。

Abstract: We investigate the prospects for experimentally distinguishing the Next-to-Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (N2HDM) and the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model with a Complex Singlet (2HDMS) in the Yukawa type II realization. Both models can successfully accommodate the reported Higgs-boson excesses around 95 GeV while satisfying current theoretical and experimental constraints, leading to very similar predictions for the observed Higgs spectrum and signal strengths. We analyze how they can nevertheless be discriminated through observables sensitive to the scalar potential and the CP-odd sector. Analytical expressions for the trilinear Higgs couplings are derived, exhibiting characteristic contributions induced by the additional cubic interactions of the 2HDMS. We study the corresponding phenomenology in two complementary collider environments: four-top production at the High-Luminosity LHC, probing the different pseudoscalar sectors. In a second step we analyze Higgs pair production at a future high-energy $e^+e^-$ collider, providing direct sensitivity to the trilinear Higgs couplings. We quantify the potential to distinguish the two models by introducing the minimum deviation from the N2HDM limit required for a $2σ$ difference between the two models. Although our numerical analysis focuses on benchmark scenarios describing the 95 GeV excesses, the proposed approach provides a general framework for discriminating between singlet-extended Higgs sectors at future colliders.

</details>


### [109] [Compositeness relations for near-threshold p-wave bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25744)
*Guo-Ying Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Weinberg的复合性关系推广到近阈值p波束缚态，并推导了p波有效程展开参数、结合能B与场重整化常数Z之间的关系，同时给出了适用于纯分子态或基本多夸克态的费曼规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Weinberg的复合性关系推广至p波束缚态，以统一描述近阈值强子态（如分子态或多夸克态）的性质。

Method: 基于有效范围展开和场论方法，推广Weinberg复合性关系，并推导相关参数间的解析关系及通用费曼规则。

Result: 得到了p波近阈值束缚态下有效范围参数、结合能B与场重整化常数Z之间的定量关系，并给出普适费曼规则。

Conclusion: 该推广为区分和刻画不同起源的近阈值强子态（分子态 vs. 多夸克态）提供了理论工具，增强了对QCD奇异强子谱的理解。

Abstract: We generalize Weinberg's compositeness relations to near-threshold p-wave bound states and derive the relations between the p-wave effective range expansion parameters, binding energy B, and the field renormalization constant Z. We also provide the corresponding Feynman rules which are appliecable regardless of whether the near-threshold state is a pure molecular state or an elementary multiquark state.

</details>


### [110] [Heavy and heavy-light mesons with arbitrary spin and parity in the Covariant Spectator Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25747)
*Alfred Stadler,Elmar P. Biernat*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将协变旁观者理论（CST）推广至任意自旋宇称J^P的夸克-反夸克介子，并改进相互作用核以包含强耦合常数的动量依赖性；仅用8个可调参数，全局拟合重及重轻介子质量谱，成功解释已知态并预测未观测态及量子数归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一描述任意自旋宇称的夸克-反夸克介子谱，需推广原有单通道CST框架并改进相互作用核的物理真实性。

Method: 推广一通道协变旁观者理论（CST）至任意J^P介子；引入动量依赖的强耦合常数以改进夸克-反夸克相互作用核；对0^±, 1^±, 2^±, 3^±介子质量进行全局拟合。

Result: 仅用8个参数即实现对重与重轻介子质量谱的高精度全局拟合；预测未测量态；支持B_c(1P)^+候选态为0^+, 1^+, 2^+，B_c^{*+}为最低1^-态，D_{s1}(2933)^+为轴矢量态。

Conclusion: 推广后的CST框架兼具简洁性与强解释力，为介子谱学提供可靠理论工具，并对若干新实验态的量子数给出有力理论支持。

Abstract: This work generalizes the one-channel Covariant Spectator Theory (CST) formalism to describe quark-antiquark mesons of arbitrary spin-parity $J^P$. We also improve the quark-antiquark interaction kernel by incorporating the momentum dependence of the strong coupling. Within this framework, we perform global fits to the masses of experimentally established heavy and heavy-light mesons with $J^P=0^\pm, 1^\pm, 2^\pm,$ and $3^\pm$. With only eight adjustable parameters, the model yields an excellent global description of the observed quark-antiquark spectrum and predicts both unmeasured states and likely $J^P$ assignments for states with unconfirmed quantum numbers. In particular, our results support the identifications of the recently observed $B_c(1P)^+$ candidates as $0^+$, $1^+$, and $2^+$ states, and the $B_c^{*+}$ as the lowest $1^-$ state in the bottom-charm sector, as well as the $D_{s1}(2933)^+$ as axial-vector state in the charm-strange sector.

</details>


### [111] [A dispersive method to study CP asymmetries in hadronic multi-body $B$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25764)
*L. A. Heuser,A. Reyes-Torrecilla,C. Hanhart,Y. Huang,B. Kubis,P. C. Magalhães,T. Mannel,J. R. Peláez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于两体末态相互作用普适性的色散方法，用于构建强子多体B介子衰变振幅，并成功解释了B±→K±π+π−中观测到的局域CP破坏现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LHCb实验中观测到的B±→K±π+π−衰变中增强的局域CP破坏现象，厘清其物理起源（是CP破坏增强还是CP守恒部分被压制）。

Method: 采用基于低不变质量下成对强子末态相互作用普适性的色散方法，将振幅分解为由唯象参数控制的源项和由精确已知两体动力学（共振与非共振）主导的末态相互作用项；以精确的ππ低能相互作用为输入，拟合角积分CP不对称度数据。

Result: 成功预测了Dalitz图上事件分布及CP不对称度的微分分布；揭示该局域CP破坏主要源于CP守恒部分受多个分波干涉导致的压制，而非CP破坏效应的绝对增强；指出常被忽略的非共振同位旋-2贡献对描述该系统CP破坏起关键作用。

Conclusion: 该色散方法不仅合理解释了B±→K±π+π−中的局域CP破坏，还表明非共振同位旋-2分波至关重要；该框架可推广至其他由两强子末态相互作用驱动CP破坏的多体衰变过程。

Abstract: We present the details of a dispersive method to construct the amplitudes for hadronic multibody $B$ decays based on the universality of pairwise hadronic final-state interactions at low invariant masses. This approach allows us to split the amplitude into source terms controlled by phenomenological parameters and final-state interactions, governed by the precise knowledge of two-body dynamics, both resonant and non-resonant. As a concrete application, we make use of the well-determined low-energy pion-pion ($ππ$) interactions to understand the enhanced localized CP violation observed in $B^{\pm}\rightarrow K^{\pm}π^+π^-$. Fitting only angle-integrated CP-asymmetry data, the method successfully predicts the Dalitz plot differential distribution of events and of the CP asymmetry in the low-energy region. This allows for a better understanding of the large localized CP asymmetry recently reported by LHCb, which is shown not to be driven by an absolute enhancement of CP-violating effects, but by a suppression of the CP-conserving part resulting from the interplay of several partial waves. Moreover, we show that the widely-neglected non-resonant isospin-2 contributions play an essential role in the description of CP violation in this system. The method can be straightforwardly adapted to other multibody decays, where final-state two-hadron interactions drive the CP violation.

</details>


### [112] [Probing the limits on anomalous quartic gauge couplings via $ZZγ$ production in the $\ell\ellννγ$ channel at FCC-hh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25935)
*A. Yilmaz,A. Senol,H. Denizli,I. Turk Cakir,O. Cakir*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用FCC-hh对pp→ZZγ过程在ℓℓννγ末态的模拟，通过三种多变量分析方法（含深度神经网络）探测反常四规范玻色子耦合（aQGCs），在30 ab⁻¹积分亮度下给出f_T0、f_T8、f_T9和f_M2等算符的95%置信度上限，灵敏度比当前LHC结果提升约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高对反常四规范玻色子耦合（aQGCs）的探测灵敏度，以探索超出标准模型的新物理；FCC-hh的超高能量与亮度为实现更严格限制提供了可能。

Method: 基于FCC-hh 100 TeV质子-质子对撞机环境，采用真实探测器模拟，分析pp→ZZγ → ℓℓννγ（ℓ=e/μ）过程；使用三种多变量方法（含深度神经网络）进行信背分离，并施加基于总横质量M_T^tot的幺正性约束。

Result: 在无系统误差假设下，对|f_T0/Λ⁴|、|f_T8/Λ⁴|、|f_T9/Λ⁴|和|f_M2/Λ⁴|的95% CL限制分别为2.83×10⁻³、1.65×10⁻³、3.81×10⁻³和8.97×10⁻³ TeV⁻⁴；DNN方法表现最优；考虑5%系统误差时，灵敏度相较当前LHC结果提升约一个数量级。

Conclusion: FCC-hh在ZZγ道中对aQGCs具有显著更强的探测能力，验证了高能未来对撞机在探索电弱对称性破缺新物理中的关键作用。

Abstract: In this study, the sensitivity to anomalous quartic gauge couplings (aQGCs) is projected via $pp \rightarrow ZZγ$ production in the 100 TeV proton-proton Future Circular Collider - hadron-hadron (FCC-hh) for an integrated luminosity of 30 ab$^{-1}$. The $\ell\ellννγ$ final state under consideration consists of a same-flavor, opposite-sign lepton pair (electrons or muons) from one $Z$ boson, the invisible decay of the other $Z$ boson into neutrinos, and an accompanying photon. The FCC-hh detector response and its effects on the reconstructed objects are included through a realistic detector simulation. Three multivariate techniques are employed to separate the signal from the relevant SM backgrounds. Unitarity is preserved by a strict, operator-dependent bound on the total transverse mass ($M_T^{tot}$) of the system. The median expected significances are calculated within the Asimov approximation for one anomalous coupling varied at a time and for background systematic uncertainties between 0\% and 10\%. The highest separation power is obtained with the deep neural network method. The resulting 95\% confidence level limits on $|f_{T0}/Λ^{4}|$, $|f_{T8}/Λ^{4}|$, $|f_{T9}/Λ^{4}|$ and $|f_{M2}/Λ^{4}|$ in the combined $e+μ$ channel without systematic uncertainties are $2.83\times 10^{-3}$, $1.65\times 10^{-3}$, $3.81\times 10^{-3}$ and $8.97\times 10^{-3}$ TeV$^{-4}$, respectively. We have an order of magnitude improvement when compared to current LHC limits with the assumption of 5\% systematic uncertainty.

</details>


### [113] [Probing Lepton-Flavor-Violating Four-Lepton Operators at a Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26020)
*Sukanta Dutta,Purnath Unnikrishnan,Yashasvi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在高能μ子对撞机上，通过标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）中维度六的四轻子算符诱导的带电轻子味破坏（LFV）过程，利用角分布最优观测量分析和全局拟合，获得了比当前限制高一个数量级的威尔逊系数灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越标准模型的新物理信号，特别是带电轻子味破坏现象，以检验标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）参数空间。

Method: 在3、10、14 TeV质心能量下，模拟μ⁺μ⁻→e±τ∓、μ⁺μ⁻→e±μ∓、μ⁺μ⁻→μ±τ∓等LFV过程；考虑束流极化与强子型τ重建；采用基于角分布的最优观测量进行多算符全局拟合。

Result: 对四轻子算符的威尔逊系数与截断能标平方之比C/Λ²达到(0.6–1.6)×10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻²的投影灵敏度，优于现有实验限制约一个数量级；联合多能量与极化数据显著改善威尔逊系数间相关性的分辨能力。

Conclusion: 多TeV级μ子对撞机对SMEFT中带电LFV四轻子相互作用具有极强探测量，是探索新物理的重要前沿平台。

Abstract: We investigate charged lepton-flavour violation (LFV) induced by dimension-six four-lepton operators within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory at a proposed high-energy muon collider. We study the processes $μ^{+}μ^{-}\to e^{\pm}τ^{\mp}$, $μ^{+}μ^{-}\to e^{\pm}μ^{\mp}$, and $μ^{+}μ^{-}\to μ^{\pm}τ^{\mp}$ at $\sqrt{s}=3$, $10$, and $14$~TeV, incorporating beam polarisation and hadronic $τ$ reconstruction. Using an optimal-observable analysis of the angular distributions, we perform a global fit to the relevant set of four-lepton operators. Projected sensitivities reach $C/Λ^{2}\sim(0.6$-$1.6)\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$, depending on the flavour and chiral structure of the operator, exceeding current limits by up to an order of magnitude. A combined analysis of multiple centre-of-mass energies and beam polarisations significantly improves the resolution of correlations among the Wilson coefficients. These results highlight the strong sensitivity of a future multi-TeV muon collider to charged lepton flavour violating four-lepton interactions, establishing it as a powerful probe of the SMEFT parameter space.

</details>

### [114] [Tidal deformation of an accreting compact object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24938)
*Avijit Chowdhury,Chiranjeeb Singha,Kazuharu Bamba,Sumanta Chakraborty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在吸积盘环境中，类史瓦西型奇异致密天体（ECOs）的静态形变率，发现吸积盘虽显著增强响应幅度，但不改变其对致密性对数依赖的核心特征，表明环境效应虽强但仍可能区分ECOs与黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密天体的潮汐形变是探测强引力场和天体本质的敏感探针；现实中致密天体常处于物质丰富的环境（如吸积盘），环境可能显著改变其响应，需系统研究。

Method: 采用相对论性薄盘解建模外部时空，解析研究嵌入准稳态、自引力薄吸积盘中的类史瓦西奇异致密天体（ECOs）的静态可变形性，分离环境对标量和自旋-1响应的贡献。

Result: 对于完美反射的ECOs，其标量和自旋-1响应对致密性的特征对数依赖关系（由近视界物理决定）在吸积存在下依然保持；吸积盘主要显著放大响应的整体幅度。

Conclusion: 环境效应（如吸积盘）会显著影响潮汐信号，但在适当条件下，仍可通过引力波观测区分无事件视界的致密天体与黑洞。

Abstract: Tidal deformation of a compact object serves as a sensitive probe of the strong-gravity regime and nature of the compact object. It captures how a compact object responds to the external perturbing field of a companion. In realistic astrophysical settings, compact objects are typically immersed in matter-rich environments, which can significantly alter the response. In this work, we investigate the static deformability of Schwarzschild-like exotic compact objects (ECOs) embedded in a quasi-stationary, self-gravitating thin accretion disk. By modelling the external spacetime with a relativistic thin-disk solution, we isolate environmental contributions to the scalar and spin-1 response while maintaining analytical control. We show that, for perfectly reflecting ECOs, the characteristic logarithmic dependence of the scalar and spin-1 response on compactness, set by near-horizon physics, remains intact even in the presence of accretion. The disk primarily amplifies the overall magnitude of the response significantly. These findings highlight that environmental effects can seriously impact tidal signatures, while still permitting, under suitable conditions, the distinguishability of horizonless compact objects from black holes in gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [115] [Fast, accurate, and differentiable: a neural-network surrogate for NRSur7dq4 precessing binary black hole waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24960)
*Michael Pürrer,Ashwin Girish,Lucy M. Thomas,Scott E. Field,Vijay Varma*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的代理模型，用于高效、准确地替代NRSur7dq4引力波形模型，支持GPU加速与自动微分，适用于引力波参数估计等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升预进动双黑洞并合引力波形建模的计算效率与可微性，以支持实时参数估计和梯度驱动的贝叶斯推断方法。

Method: 将NRSur7dq4波形分解为多个物理量，对每个量分别训练独立的多层感知机（MLP）；整个模型用JAX实现，支持GPU加速与端到端自动微分。

Result: 在全参数空间验证下，频域平均失配中位数为8.0×10⁻⁵至1.7×10⁻⁴，95%分位低于10⁻³；单波形GPU推理耗时约1 ms，比LALSimulation快约10倍，批量吞吐量高约140倍。

Conclusion: 这是首个兼具数值相对论保真精度、完全可微分性和GPU加速能力的预进动NR波形代理模型，为基于梯度的引力波参数估计开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We present a neural network surrogate model that emulates the NRSur7dq4 gravitational waveform model for precessing binary black hole mergers. The surrogate decomposes the waveform into constituent quantities and trains an independent multilayer perceptron (MLP) for each. We validate the surrogate against NRSur7dq4 on 10,000 waveforms spanning its full parameter space ($1 \leq q \leq 4$, $|χ_{A,B}| \leq 0.8$). For representative total masses between 60 and 300 $M_\odot$, median sky-averaged frequency-domain mismatches range from $8.0 \times 10^{-5}$ to $1.7 \times 10^{-4}$, with 95th percentiles below $10^{-3}$. On an NVIDIA L40S GPU the JAX surrogate evaluates a single waveform in about 1 ms end-to-end, roughly 10 times faster than the LALSimulation C implementation of NRSur7dq4, and sustains about 140 times the LALSimulation throughput at batch size 64, making it well suited for both low-latency parameter-estimation samplers and large-scale waveform generation. The full NRSur7dq4 NN waveform-to-likelihood pipeline is implemented in JAX and is differentiable. This is the first neural-network surrogate of a precessing numerical-relativity waveform model to combine validated NR-faithful accuracy with a fully differentiable, GPU-accelerated inference pipeline, enabling gradient-based inference approaches via automatic differentiation including Fisher information matrices, GPU-accelerated nested sampling, gradient-based MCMC and importance sampling.

</details>


### [116] [Coupled Gravitoelectromagnetic Response of a Magnetically Supported Generalized Hayward Black Hole in Nonlinear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24982)
*Anirudh Pradhan,K. Ghaderi,M. Zeyauddin,A. Gulhane*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了磁支撑广义Hayward黑洞在外场耦合引力与电磁响应下的理论框架，通过非线性电动力学补全模型，系统研究其拟正规模谱、度规与光学特性、极值喉权重、四极波转换等关键物理量，并验证了外域双曲性与通量守恒；结果表明，在弱核心几何下可由有效带电算符主导，残余修正分别呈立方（电磁主导分支）与四次（引力主导分支）特征，且多个独立可观测量一致反映同一非线性电动力学算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非线性电动力学背景下广义Hayward黑洞的外部耦合引力与电磁响应特性，以理解其在强场极限下的物理行为及与标准黑洞模型（如Schwarzschild和Reissner–Nordström）的关联。

Method: 基于指定的非线性电动力学补全模型，构建磁支撑广义Hayward黑洞的外域耦合引力-电磁响应方程；将两个宇称通道约化为具有显式势函数的标准双通道波动系统；数值与解析结合计算拟正规模谱、散射特性、波转换率、吸收本征通道等，并验证双曲性与通量守恒。

Result: 获得完整的拟正规模谱、度规与光学特征、极值喉权重、四极波转换率（如χ=0.9时正宇称反射转换率达45.8%）、有限带宽传输与高精度相干吸收（亮吸收达98.4%，暗吸收仅1.3%）；发现四极分支在χ≈0.70附近出现本征线宽分离，宇称分裂则始终次主导；度规与光学族随极端性单调分离；残余修正呈现电磁分支立方、引力分支四次的谱结构特征。

Conclusion: 该工作证实非线性电动力学对黑洞外部响应具有系统性、可观测的影响，多个独立物理观测量一致指向同一非线性算符，为检验替代理论提供了新诊断工具。

Abstract: We construct the exterior coupled gravitational and electromagnetic response of a magnetically supported generalized Hayward black hole within a specified nonlinear electrodynamic completion. Both parity sectors reduce to canonical two channel wave systems with explicit potentials and the correct Schwarzschild and effective Reissner--Nordström limits. We compute the quasinormal spectrum, metric and optical characteristics, extremal throat weights, quadrupolar wave conversion, finite bandwidth transfer, and coherent absorption eigenchannels while verifying exterior hyperbolicity and flux conservation. An effective charged operator captures the leading weak core geometry and mode mixing; after its subtraction, the residual hierarchy is consistent with cubic spectral corrections in the primarily electromagnetic branch and quartic corrections in the primarily gravitational branch and principal scattering observables. The fundamental quadrupolar branches become intrinsically linewidth separated near $χ\simeq0.70$, whereas parity splitting remains subleading. Metric and optical characteristic families separate monotonically toward extremality. At $χ=0.9$, positive parity reflected conversion reaches $45.8\%$ and remains $40.8\%$ after moderate bandwidth averaging, while coherent incident combinations yield $98.4\%$ bright and $1.3\%$ dark absorption. These independently evaluated observables provide mutually consistent diagnostics of the same nonlinear electrodynamic operator.

</details>


### [117] [Fixing IR tail of gravitational waves from domain walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25073)
*Ivan Dankovsky,Dmitry Gorbunov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文讨论了早期宇宙中物质不均匀性剧烈演化产生的引力波（GW）数值模拟谱中出现的非物理红外振荡问题，并提出了一种通用的数值平滑方法来恢复真实谱，强调该方法需在GW产生结束后延长模拟几个哈勃时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值模拟得到的引力波谱红外部分存在非物理振荡，这些振荡源于平方振幅平均中的寄生双频项，必须消除；而红外斜率对观测（如NANOGrav）至关重要，需准确预测。

Method: 基于CosmoLattice代码对畴壁网络进行数值模拟，并提出一种扩展模拟时间（数个哈勃时间）以平滑寄生振荡的通用数值程序；同时检验了PRS标度法导致错误谱的问题。

Result: 成功通过延长模拟时间消除了非物理红外振荡，恢复了真实的引力波谱；验证了PRS人工标度法即使正确重标度源项也会给出错误谱。

Conclusion: 寄生红外振荡必须被平滑处理，所提出的延时模拟方法是通用且可靠的，适用于其他早期宇宙引力波源的数值模拟；而基于方程分项标度的加速模拟方法可能引入系统误差。

Abstract: Numerical simulations of gravitational waves (GW) production during violent evolution of matter inhomogeneities in the early Universe yield highly wiggle infrared parts of the spectra. Obtained directly from the two-point correlation function, these wiggles are nonphysical, corresponding to the parasitic, double frequency terms in naively averaged squared oscillation amplitudes of GW, and hence must be washed out. The deep infrared behavior can be predicted on general grounds, e.g. fixed by causality considerations. However, what matters for real observations, e.g. like that of NANOGrav, and what can be only inferred from numerical simulations, is the infrared slope near the maximum of the spectrum. It reflects the dynamics responsible for the GW production in its heyday, and hence must be accurately predicted. We illustrate the problem with numerical simulations of the Domain Wall network performed with the help of code CosmoLattice. We suggest a numerical procedure to smooth out these parasitic wiggles, which allows us to recover the true spectrum. Being quite generic, it may be applied to numerical simulations of other hypothetical sources of GW possibly operating in the early Universe. The procedure requires to extend the simulation by a few Hubble times after termination of the GW production. We checked with numerical simulations, that the technically natural long-time extension of simulations, which becomes available via artificial scaling of different parts in the scalar sector equations provided by PRS prescription, gives wrong GW spectra even if the source terms are properly rescaled.

</details>


### [118] [GSpyNetTree-O4: an event validation tool used in the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25208)
*Sofia Alvarez-Lopez,Man Leong Chan,Franz S. Herbst,Dhatri Raghunathan,Airene Ahuja,Annudesh Liyanage,Julian Ding,Alejandro Garcia-Varela,Raymond Ng,Jess McIver*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GSpyNetTree-O4，一种用于引力波探测器数据中瞬态噪声（glitch）分类与事件验证的新工具，其核心改进在于能同时识别glitch和引力波信号，并在O4运行中成功部署，显著提升了数据质量评估与事件验证的自动化水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器数据中频繁出现的非高斯瞬态噪声（glitches）会干扰引力波搜寻、参数估计及后续分析，亟需及时识别与缓解靠近候选事件的瞬态噪声。

Method: 开发了新架构的GSpyNetTree-O4模型，支持输入中glitch与引力波信号共存时的联合识别；扩充并增强训练集（含模拟引力波叠加真实glitch样本）；应用60 Hz校准修正以匹配O4实际数据；使用Q变换生成时频输入，并评估不同Q值及未见glitch形态下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在测试数据上，低质量、高质量和极高质量glitch分类器对glitch的识别率分别为97.9%、97.7%和95.4%；对无glitch样本（含纯引力波与无glitch类）的正确判定率分别为97.1%、96.6%和96.0%；在Virgo O4实测glitch及不同Q值下均表现出良好鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GSpyNetTree-O4已成功作为数据质量报告工具部署于第四次观测运行，有效增强了引力波事件验证流程的自动化能力与可靠性。

Abstract: The frequent presence of non-Gaussian transient noise, or glitches, in gravitational-wave detector data can affect gravitational-wave searches, parameter estimation, and downstream analyses. To identify and mitigate transient noise near gravitational-wave candidates in a timely manner, the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration employs the Data Quality Report. In the fourth observing run, GSpyNetTree-O4 was deployed within this framework as a tool for glitch classification and event validation. We describe GSpyNetTree-O4 and the main developments relative to its predecessor, GSpyNetTree. The most important update was a new architecture that allowed the simultaneous identification of glitches and gravitational-wave signals when both were present in the same input. We also expanded and augmented the training set with examples in which simulated gravitational-wave signals overlapped with real glitches, and applied $60\,\mathrm{Hz}$ calibration corrections to better match the data expected during the fourth observing run. On test data, the low-mass, high-mass, and extremely high-mass classifiers identified $97.9\%$, $97.7\%$, and $95.4\%$ of glitches, respectively. Among samples without a glitch, including gravitational-wave-only and No Glitch samples, the classifiers correctly reported no data-quality issues in $97.1\%$, $96.6\%$, and $96.0\%$ of cases, respectively. We further assessed the robustness of GSpyNetTree-O4 on unseen glitch morphologies, a small set of Virgo glitches from the fourth observing run, and different choices of the $Q$-value used to construct the time-frequency inputs. GSpyNetTree-O4 was successfully deployed as a Data Quality Report tool and increased automation in gravitational-wave event validation workflows.

</details>


### [119] [Parametrized beyond-Teukolsky framework in the time domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25311)
*Ciro De Simone,Sebastian H. Völkel,Kostas D. Kokkotas,Salvatore Capozziello*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个参数化超越Teukolsky框架的时间域实现，通过(2+1)维散射实验研究修正引力下黑洞扰动的完整线性演化过程，并分析了对准正常模、波形振幅与相位、晚期尾项等的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论的修正可能显著改变黑洞的微扰响应，但此前对环down阶段的研究仅限于频域本征值问题，缺乏时间域的完整演化分析。

Method: 开发了参数化超越Teukolsky框架的时间域实现，开展(2+1)维高斯波包散射实验；结合频域本征值结果进行验证，并利用时域波形估计线性化修正势模型中的二次与混合系数。

Result: 低方位量子数下时频域结果高度一致；高方位量子数受模混合影响；变形参数显著影响环down振幅与相位；近视界变形不改变晚期尾项幂律指数，但推迟其起始时刻。

Conclusion: 时域演化为检验广义相对论在黑洞微扰理论中的参数化偏离提供了互补且灵活的框架。

Abstract: Modifications to General Relativity can significantly alter the perturbative response of black holes, leaving imprints on quasinormal-mode spectra, waveform amplitudes and phases, and late-time tails. The parametrized beyond-Teukolsky framework was introduced to capture possible deviations from Kerr dynamics, but the ringdown has so far only been explored as an eigenvalue problem. We present the first time-domain implementation of this framework and perform (2+1)-dimensional scattering experiments with Gaussian wave packets. This approach provides the full linear evolution of the perturbation, from the initial prompt response through the ringdown and into the late-time regime. Using frequency-domain eigenvalue results as benchmarks, we find excellent agreement for low azimuthal numbers, while higher azimuthal numbers are affected by mode mixing, which limits the precision of the extracted modes. We further use the time-domain waveforms to estimate quadratic and mixed coefficients within the linearized modified-potential model, providing a diagnostic for the regime of validity of the linear approximation. Beyond mode frequencies, we show that the deformation parameters can strongly affect the ringdown amplitude and phase, with trends that can be understood from the near-horizon structure of the modified potential. We also analyze the late- time behavior, finding that near-horizon deformations leave the tail exponent unchanged but can substantially shift the onset of the power-law decay. These results demonstrate that time-domain evolutions provide a complementary and flexible framework for testing parametrized deviations from General Relativity in black-hole perturbation theory.

</details>


### [120] [Extracting Energy from a Non-Kerr Rotating Spacetime with an Anomalous Quadrupole Moment via Magnetic Reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25315)
*Ke Wang,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过磁重联机制从具有异常四极矩的非克尔旋转时空提取能量，并分析了能量提取的可行性、功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何从具有异常四极矩且存在闭合类时曲线的非克尔旋转时空中提取能量，同时规避这些非物理区域。

Method: 分析该时空的事件视界、能层和闭合类时曲线，并计算加速与减速等离子体在无穷远处的单位焓能量、能量提取允许区域、功率及效率。

Result: 正负异常四极矩均支持能量提取，但小的正异常四极矩对应更高的提取功率与效率。

Conclusion: 磁重联机制可在排除闭合类时曲线区域的前提下，有效实现该类非克尔时空的能量提取，且正的小异常四极矩最有利。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to extract energy from a non-Kerr rotating spacetime with an anomalous quadrupole moment via the magnetic reconnection mechanism. Unlike many other rotating spacetimes, this spacetime possesses closed timelike curves, and the corresponding spacetime regions must be excluded when extracting energy. After introducing the event horizon, ergosphere, and closed timelike curves of this spacetime, we deeply analyze the energy per unit enthalpy at infinity for accelerated and decelerated plasmas, the allowed region for energy extraction, and the power and efficiency of energy extraction. The results show that energy extraction is possible for both positive and negative anomalous quadrupole moments, but a positive and small anomalous quadrupole moment corresponds to higher power and efficiency of energy extraction.

</details>


### [121] [Memory Effect for deformed gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25452)
*P. -M. Zhang,M. Elbistan,P. A. Horvathy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了三明治引力波的形变，发现当将纯位移参数（DM）的波挤压成冲量波时，DM特性会消失，仅保留速度效应（VM）；而通过增大振幅可对每个固定形变参数p恢复DM特性，但该振幅在p趋于无穷时发散，故无法得到真正的冲量波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三明治引力波在形变过程中DM与VM特性的演化关系及其极限行为。

Method: 理论分析三明治引力波在形变参数p下的DM/VM特性变化，考察振幅随p的变化趋势及极限行为。

Result: 形变为冲量波时DM特性丢失，仅存VM；对固定p可通过增大振幅恢复DM，但该振幅随p→∞而发散。

Conclusion: 纯位移型三明治引力波无法通过有限振幅形变为严格冲量波，DM与VM特性存在本质不可调和性。

Abstract: Deformations of sandwich gravitational waves are considered. Squeezing a wave with pure displacement (DM) parameters to an impulsive wave, the DM property is lost, leaving us with the velocity effect (VM). For each fixed value of the deformation parameter $p$, DM can be restored by further increasing the amplitude which however diverge when $p\to\infty$, and no impulsive wave is obtained.

</details>


### [122] [Gravitational Baryogenesis in Rainbow Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25491)
*P. Goodarzi,H. Mohseni Sadjadi,R. Darabi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在彩虹引力修正下的正则化四维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内模型中的引力重子生成机制，发现该框架可在辐射主导时期产生非零的里奇标量时间导数，并通过解析和数值方法得出与观测一致的重子-熵比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准宇宙学中辐射主导时期重子不对称性生成困难的问题，探索高阶引力理论与彩虹引力结合对重子生成的影响。

Method: 构建正则化四维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内引力模型并引入彩虹引力修正，推导里奇标量时间导数行为，分别在高曲率与微扰区域解析求解重子-熵比，并进行数值拟合。

Result: 高斯-博内项在辐射时期产生非零重子不对称性（而爱因斯坦-希尔伯特项在此时期为零），数值分析给出与观测值一致的参数空间。

Conclusion: 该组合框架能自然解释观测到的重子不对称性，为早期宇宙重子生成提供新机制。

Abstract: The gravitational baryogenesis is studied in regularized four dimensional Einstein Gauss Bonnet model with rainbow gravity corrections. This combined framework generates a non vanishing time derivative of the Ricci scalar even during radiation domination era. We derive analytical expressions for the baryon to entropy ratio in both the high curvature and perturbative regimes. The Gauss Bonnet contribution produces nonzero asymmetry precisely at radiation era, where the Einstein Hilbert contribution vanishes. Numerical analysis identifies viable parameter spaces reproducing the observed baryon to entropy ratio, compatible with observations.

</details>


### [123] [Gravitational wave constraints on corrections to Bekenstein-Hawking Area Formula in classical F(R) gravity and quantum GR : implications for theory parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25513)
*Parthasarathi Majumdar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LVK联盟对引力波数据（特别是GW250114事件）的分析结果，结合霍金面积定理的5σ验证，约束了F(R)引力理论及圈量子引力/纠缠熵框架下黑洞熵的面积修正项，并由此限制了超出标准模型粒子（如暗物质候选者）的自旋与种类数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过引力波观测验证霍金面积定理，进而约束黑洞熵公式可能的修正形式，以检验量子引力理论和修正引力理论。

Method: 基于LVK引力波数据分析结果（GW250114，SNR≈80，5σ确认面积定理），结合贝肯斯坦黑洞熵思想与广义热力学第二定律，运用Wald熵函数形式，引入‘绝对一致性’判据，约束F(R)引力中的逆面积修正项；并整合圈量子引力与纠缠熵给出的对数修正项，施加绝对一致性条件。

Result: 得到F(R)引力参数间的关联关系；并对超出标准模型粒子（含潜在暗物质候选者）的自旋和种类数施加显著限制。

Conclusion: 引力波观测已具备约束量子引力与修正引力中黑洞熵修正的能力，且对基本粒子物理参数（如暗物质性质）具有间接但重要的限制作用。

Abstract: This contribution considers constraints from analyses of gravitational wave data from binary black hole coalescence, on possible corrections to the Bekenstein-Hawking Area Formula for black hole entropy. Most recent analyses of gravitational wave data from the LVK Consortium appear to confirm the Hawking Area Theorem for black holes at a $5 σ$ accuracy, for the `loudest' signal (SNR of the order of $80$) of binary black hole merger inherent in the recent observation GW250114. Amalgamating this result with Bekenstein's ideas of black hole entropy and the generalized second law of thermodynamics, we constrain leading inverse area corrections for large horizon area spherical black hole solutions of classical $F(R)$ gravity, using the Wald entropy function formalism. The implementation of the observational constraints entails the notion of `absolute consistency' which we introduce and contrast with `relative consistency'. This absolute consistency criterion is shown to relate some of the parameters of $F(R)$ gravity. Next we consider leading quantum general relativistic corrections to the Area formula, arising both from the non-perturbative matter-free Loop Quantum Gravity and matter-dependent, perturbative Entanglement entropy approaches. Combining the leading logarithmic corrections in horizon area (for large areas) from both approaches, and imposing absolute consistency with the observational validation of the Area Theorem, is shown to lead to significant restrictions on the spin and number of species of Beyond Standard Model spectrum of elementary particles, some of which are often assumed to be Dark Matter candidates.

</details>


### [124] [Spin precession in the strong deflection limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25617)
*Jiafei Geng,Yunchuan Xiang,Qingquan Jiang,Xiankai Pang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了静态球对称渐近平直时空下粒子沿测地线运动时自旋进动角的强偏折极限（SDL）展开式，建立了进动角与偏折角之间的简单关系，并应用于Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström时空，验证了无质量粒子后向散射中的普适自旋翻转现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强偏折极限下光线偏折已有成熟理论，但自旋进动缺乏系统性的SDL处理。

Method: 推导静态、球对称、渐近平直时空下测地线粒子自旋进动角的强偏折极限展开，计算SDL系数，并建立其与偏折角的关系；应用于Schwarzschild与Reissner-Nordström时空，分别给出解析结果与电荷修正项。

Result: 获得Schwarzschild时空下完全解析的SDL系数，以及Reissner-Nordström时空下至O(Q²)的电荷修正；证实无质量粒子后向散射中普适自旋翻转，揭示‘荣耀斑’缺失的机制；发现有质量粒子的进动具有非平凡电荷依赖性。

Conclusion: 自旋进动在强偏折极限下可系统展开，其与偏折角存在简洁关联；无质量粒子进动与电荷无关，而有质量粒子则能区分RN与Schwarzschild时空，为引力探测提供新视角。

Abstract: The strong deflection limit (SDL) of the deflection angle is well established for general spherically symmetric spacetimes, but a systematic SDL treatment of spin precession has been lacking. We derive the SDL expansion for the spin precession angle of particles propagating along geodesics in static, spherically symmetric, and asymptotically flat spacetimes. The SDL coefficients are obtained, and a simple relation linking the precession angle to the deflection one is established. Applying the formalism to Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström (RN) spacetimes, we obtain fully analytic SDL coefficients for the former and perturbative charge corrections to $\mathcal{O}(Q^2)$ for the latter. These results explicitly verify the universal spin-flip of massless particles in backward scattering, which is the underlying mechanism governing the absence of the glory spot. While the spin precession of massless particles is charge-independent, massive particles acquire a non-trivial charge dependence that distinguishes the RN case from Schwarzschild.

</details>


### [125] [Conserved Gravitational Charges as Curvature Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25646)
*Emel Altas,Bayram Tekin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将守恒引力荷表示为线性化曲率通量的新方法，在渐近AdS和渐近平坦时空下均适用；通过构造一个散度为零的四阶张量，结合Killing势等技术，统一导出了包括ADM能量动量、角动量及质心/Boost电荷在内的多种守恒量，并在Schwarzschild、Kerr等精确解上验证了归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力荷通常以度规扰动及其一阶导数的边界积分形式表达，缺乏几何直观且难以推广；作者旨在寻找更本质、基于曲率的等价表达，尤其希望统一处理渐近AdS与渐近平坦情形，并克服Λ→0极限失效的问题。

Method: 引入一个迹正比于宇宙学爱因斯坦张量的散度自由四阶张量；在AdS背景下复现Abbott-Deser曲率表示；在平坦背景下引入反对称Poincaré Killing势，利用代数Bianchi恒等式将线性化爱因斯坦流转化为全散度，从而导出单一曲率通量公式。

Result: 获得适用于渐近AdS与渐近平坦时空的统一曲率通量型引力荷表达式；在四维下，平移部分复现ADM能量-动量，洛伦兹部分复现角动量与质心/Boost电荷；在Schwarzschild、boosted Schwarzschild和Kerr解上显式验证归一化正确；指出非极大对称Einstein背景中Weyl曲率会引入额外项，纯余维二曲率通量公式不直接成立。

Conclusion: 引力守恒荷可等价地表述为线性化曲率的通量，该几何表述更具普适性与内在一致性；Killing势的引入是连接平移与洛伦兹荷的关键技术突破；结果强化了曲率在引力守恒律中的核心地位，并为更一般背景下的荷定义提供了新思路。

Abstract: Conserved gravitational charges are commonly expressed as surface integrals of the metric perturbation and its first derivatives. We show that, in the usual asymptotically AdS and asymptotically flat settings, the standard charges admit equivalent representatives as fluxes of linearized curvature. The construction uses a divergence-free rank-four tensor whose trace is proportional to the cosmological Einstein tensor. On a maximally symmetric AdS background, it reproduces the known curvature representation of the Abbott-Deser charges. The asymptotically flat construction is not obtained by taking a naive $Λ\rightarrow 0$ limit, since the Killing two-form vanishes for translations. We instead introduce an antisymmetric Poincaré Killing potential. A representative Killing potential adapted to the algebraic Bianchi identity converts the linearized Einstein current into a total divergence and yields a single curvature-flux formula. Its translation sector reproduces the ADM energy--momentum, while in four dimensions its Lorentz sector reproduces the angular momentum and boost/center-of-mass charges under the standard Regge-Teitelboim falloff and parity conditions. The normalization is checked explicitly for Schwarzschild, boosted Schwarzschild, and Kerr data. On non-maximally symmetric Einstein backgrounds, background Weyl curvature generates additional terms, so the maximally symmetric construction does not directly extend to a pure codimension-two curvature-flux formula.

</details>


### [126] [Taub-NUT as gravitational dyon with torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25654)
*Dmitri Gal'tsov,Rostom Karsanov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出Taub-NUT度规的Levi-Civita联络在Misner弦上奇异，导致爱因斯坦张量含δ函数平方；引入挠率可改善此问题，并将爱因斯坦张量解释为具有负变张力的宇宙弦；挠率还生成Misner弦上的Komar流，从而决定渐近Komar荷；最终Taub-NUT度规被诠释为由两束自旋流体支撑的孤子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Taub-NUT度规中Levi-Civita联络在Misner弦上的奇异性及其导致的爱因斯坦张量非良定义问题。

Method: 引入挠率修正联络，分析修正后爱因斯坦张量的物理诠释，并研究Komar流与挠率的关系。

Result: 发现挠率可消除δ函数平方项，使爱因斯坦张量对应负变张力宇宙弦；挠率生成Misner弦上的Komar流并决定渐近Komar荷；Taub-NUT解被理解为两束自旋流体支撑的孤子。

Conclusion: Taub-NUT几何需借助挠率才能获得一致的物理诠释，其本质是挠率驱动的自旋流体孤子结构。

Abstract: We show that the Levi-Civita connection for the Taub-NUT metric is singular on Misner strings and generates an Einstein tensor containing the squares of the delta function. This can be improved by introducing torsion, in which case the Einstein tensor may be interpreted as that of a cosmic string with negative variable tension. Komar flows in Misner strings are generated by torsion, which is therefore responsible for the asymptotic Komar charges. The Taub-NUT metric then appears as a soliton supported by two spin-fluid beams.

</details>


### [127] [Echoes and quasinormal modes for static loop quantum black bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25738)
*Huajie Gong,Guoyang Fu,Shulan Li,Jian-Pin Wu,Qin Tan,Qiyuan Pan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态圈量子黑洞反弹（LQBB）时空中的标量扰动，重点关注l=1多极指数下的时域信号和基本准正规模（QNFs），发现规则黑洞（RBH）无回声而某些可穿越虫洞存在清晰回声，QNFs结果与两种数值方法一致，并揭示了参数对衰减和回声产生的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究圈量子引力框架下LQBB时空（统一描述规则黑洞与可穿越虫洞）中波的传播特性，特别是回声信号与准正规模如何反映时空结构（如视界与喉部）的差异。

Method: 采用有限差分法模拟时域标量扰动信号，结合Prony方法和直接积分法提取准正规模（QNFs），分析不同量子参数α和反弹参数r_b下有效势的单势垒与势阱结构对回声和QNFs的影响。

Result: 规则黑洞配置下无回声（单势垒势），而特定可穿越虫洞配置出现清晰回声（势阱结构）；QNFs在两种方法下结果一致；RBH中增大r_b或α导致衰减变慢，虫洞中QNFs随参数呈非单调变化，回声出现与有效势轮廓密切相关。

Conclusion: LQBB时空为研究规则黑洞与可穿越虫洞中的波动力学提供了有效框架，有助于厘清视界与喉部结构对环down信号及回声特征的影响机制。

Abstract: We investigate scalar perturbations of the static loop quantum black bounce (LQBB) spacetime with multipole index $l=1$, focusing on time-domain signals and fundamental quasinormal frequencies (QNFs). The LQBB model provides a unified description of regular black holes (RBHs) and traversable wormholes, governed by the quantum parameter $α$ and the bounce parameter $r_b$. Using the finite difference method, we find no echoes for the displayed RBH configurations with a single-barrier effective potential, whereas clear echoes are produced by the potential well structure in selected traversable wormhole configurations. The QNFs obtained from the Prony method and the direct integration method are in good agreement. In the RBH case, increasing $r_b$ or $α$ leads to a slower decay. In the wormhole case, the QNFs depend non-monotonically on the model parameters, and the emergence of echoes is closely tied to the effective potential profile. These results show that the LQBB spacetime provides a useful framework for studying wave dynamics in RBHs and traversable wormholes, and for clarifying how horizon and throat structures affect ringdown and echoes.

</details>


### [128] [Late-Time Cosmological Tests of a Minisuperspace Generalized-Uncertainty-Principle Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25745)
*G. G. L. Nashed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了二次广义不确定性原理（GUP）对FLRW宇宙学模型相空间代数的修正，导致 Friedmann 方程的一阶修正，并利用宇宙时钟、Pantheon+ 和 DESI DR2 BAO 数据限制该修正参数 β*，结果与标准ΛCDM及wCDM模型进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力效应对宇宙学的影响，特别是其在晚期宇宙膨胀观测中的可检验性。

Method: 构建二次GUP修正的FLRW minisuperspace泊松代数，在一阶变形参数下导出修正的Friedmann方程；仅分析背景演化，联合31个宇宙时钟、1580个Pantheon+样本和DESI DR2的13组BAO数据进行贝叶斯拟合，将声速视界作为哑元参数。

Result: 得到基线归一化下GUP参数β* = -0.086^{+0.040}_{-0.032}（68%置信度），95%置信区间为(-0.142, 0.005)。

Conclusion: 该GUP修正模型在当前观测精度下与ΛCDM及wCDM兼容，但倾向于负的β*值，暗示微弱的量子引力修正效应，尚不足以显著偏离标准宇宙学。

Abstract: The investigation of possible quantum-gravity effects in cosmology is of considerable interest, especially when such effects can be tested against observations of the late-time expansion. In the present work we study a quadratic generalized-uncertainty-principle (GUP) deformation of the FLRW minisuperspace Poisson algebra. At first order in the deformation parameter, the construction modifies the Friedmann equation and hence the homogeneous expansion history. Since it does not determine a unique perturbation sector, we restrict the analysis to background observables, using 31 cosmic-chronometer measurements, the uncalibrated 1580-object Pantheon$+$ Hubble-flow sample, and the 13-component DESI DR2 baryon-acoustic-oscillation likelihood, while treating the sound horizon as a nuisance parameter. We compare the resulting cosmology with flat \LCDM{} and with the Chevallier--Polarski--Linder parametrization. For the baseline normalization we obtain $\betastar=-0.086^{+0.040}_{-0.032}$ at $68\%$ credibility and $-0.142<\betastar<0.005$ at $95\%$ credibility.

</details>


### [129] [General Relativistic Entropic Acceleration at the perturbation level: a CLASS implementation and first Boltzmann-code constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25841)
*Simone D'Onofrio,Dong Ha Lee,Eleonora Di Valentino,Juan García-Bellido*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义相对论熵加速（GREA）模型，通过因果宇宙学视界熵增长解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀，无需引入宇宙学常数；首次将其嵌入Einstein-Boltzmann求解器CLASS中，并结合CMB、BAO和超新星数据进行参数约束，发现耦合参数α≈1，与理论预期一致，且拟合优度与ΛCDM模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀现象，避免引入宇宙学常数，从热力学熵增长角度提供物理解释。

Method: 将GREA背景模型直接集成到CLASS代码中，将熵成分作为有效流体，采用参数化后弗里德曼（PPF）方案演化，结合COBAYA进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）分析，使用CMB、DESI DR2 BAO和Ia型超新星数据联合约束。

Result: 耦合参数α被约束为约1，与理论预测高度一致；模型与ΛCDM的χ²差异|Δχ²|≲6；推断的状态方程在z≈2处第二次穿越幽灵边界，是热力学动力学的特有预言。

Conclusion: GREA模型在包含扰动的完整观测检验下表现优异，支持熵驱动宇宙加速的物理图像，为暗能量问题提供了不依赖宇宙学常数的新范式。

Abstract: General Relativistic Entropic Acceleration (GREA) attributes the late-time acceleration of the Universe to the entropy growth of the causal cosmological horizon, without a cosmological constant, with a phenomenology fixed by the single $\mathcal{O}(1)$ parameter $α$. The model has so far been confronted with data only at the background level. We present its first implementation within an Einstein-Boltzmann solver: the GREA background is integrated directly into CLASS, while the entropic component is evolved as an effective fluid regulated by the parametrized-post-Friedmann scheme, giving access to the full CMB and matter power spectra. A Markov-chain Monte Carlo analysis with COBAYA against the full primary-CMB likelihoods, DESI DR2 BAO and Type Ia supernovae constrains the coupling $α\sim 1$, in excellent agreement with the theoretical prediction, with a fit matching $Λ$CDM to within $|Δχ^2| \lesssim 6$ despite the addition of a single free parameter. The equation of state inferred from the data agrees with binned, model-independent reconstructions and exhibits a second crossing of the phantom divide at $z \simeq 2$, a distinctive prediction of the thermodynamic dynamics rather than of an imposed parametrization.

</details>


### [130] [Analytic backreaction of a scalar wig on a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25932)
*Marco de Cesare,Manuel Del Piano,Carlos A. R. Herdeiro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在小耦合极限下，解析研究了球对称大质量复标量准束缚态对史瓦西黑洞的反作用，通过求解爱因斯坦方程得到度规扰动、Misner-Sharp质量演化及未来外捕获视界演化，并验证了黑洞质量随标量云衰减而单调增长并最终饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大质量标量场准束缚态对黑洞时空的反作用，尤其是考虑其指数衰减的动力学行为，而非稳态近似。

Method: 在小耦合极限（r₀μ ≪ 1）下，计算基态s波标量场的应力能张量，在ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein坐标系中将爱因斯坦方程解至振幅的二次阶；采用匹配渐近展开法解析求得准共振频率，并用Leaver方法数值验证。

Result: 获得度规扰动和Misner-Sharp质量的显式表达式；推导出未来外捕获视界的演化规律；发现黑洞质量随衰减的视界通量单调增长并最终饱和，且标量云质量减少与视界增长精确抵消；确定了标量小耦合与引力微扰展开同时有效的参数区域。

Conclusion: 标量准束缚态的动态衰减对黑洞几何有可解析刻画的非平凡反作用，其质量增长与吸收过程满足局部能量守恒，小耦合与微扰框架在特定参数域内自洽有效。

Abstract: We analytically determine the leading backreaction of a spherically symmetric massive complex scalar quasi-bound state (with mass $μ$) on a Schwarzschild black hole with (initial) gravitational radius $r_0$. Working in the small-coupling regime, $r_0 μ\ll 1$, we evaluate the stress-energy tensor of the fundamental scalar $s$-wave and solve the Einstein equations through quadratic order in its amplitude in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates. We also determine the small-mass quasi-resonant frequency of the fundamental $s$-wave analytically by matched asymptotic expansions and validate it numerically using Leaver's method. Unlike steady-state treatments, the calculation retains the exponential decay of the quasi-bound state. We obtain explicit expressions for the metric perturbations and Misner-Sharp mass and derive the evolution of the future outer trapping horizon. The black-hole mass grows monotonically with the decaying horizon flux and saturates when the finite scalar cloud has been absorbed, with the decrease of the cloud mass exactly balancing the horizon growth at the perturbative order considered. We also determine the domain in which the scalar small-coupling approximation and the gravitational perturbative expansion are simultaneously valid.

</details>

*Xi-Li Zhang,Jing Ren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了量子黑洞的时间域格林函数，阐明了不同准正规模（QNM）如何在因果框架下构建响应信号，揭示了QNM重建在不同时期的适用性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器灵敏度提升使得黑洞环降谱学测试更精确，但准正规模谱不稳定，且因果律要求早期环降信号仍需保持黑洞特征；量子黑洞存在强内部反射，其时域波形与不同QNM谱间缺乏一致图像。

Method: 通过分解时间域格林函数为因果分离的成分，并一致选择反拉普拉斯积分路径，系统分析位于光环势垒内外源点对应的量子黑洞响应，结合理论推导与时域数值模拟。

Result: 发现量子黑洞QNM重建总能忠实描述被弯曲时空探测到的信号；其实际效率依赖演化阶段：早期等价于传统黑洞QNM加尾项展开，收敛性受源位置影响；晚期则长寿命模式更高效。

Conclusion: 建立了统一的因果框架，弥合了经典黑洞谱学与量子黑洞地震学之间的理论鸿沟，为引力波数据分析提供新基础。

Abstract: The growing sensitivity of gravitational-wave detectors enables increasingly precise tests of black hole (BH) ringdown spectroscopy. BH quasinormal modes (QNMs) are, however, spectrally unstable: small near-horizon modifications can produce a drastically different QNM spectrum, while causality requires the prompt ringdown to remain BH-like until the reflected signal returns. Quantum BHs with substantial interior reflection provide a natural setting for this tension, yet the relation between their time-domain waveform and different QNM spectra still lacks a consistent picture. In this work, we systematically examine the time-domain Green function for quantum BHs, considering sources located outside and inside the light-ring potential barrier. By decomposing the Green function into causally distinct components and choosing the corresponding inverse-Laplace contours consistently, we clarify how the response is built from different sets of QNMs. We find that the quantum BH QNM reconstruction always faithfully describes the signal once the curved spacetime is probed, but its practical efficiency depends strongly on the evolutionary stage. Before interior reflection becomes relevant, we prove that this basis is formally equivalent to the BH QNM and tail expansions, with convergence properties sensitive to source location. At late times, the long-lived modes provide an efficient basis. Time-domain simulations confirm these results, providing a unified causal framework for BH spectroscopy and quantum BH seismology.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum Gravity from Fractal Entanglement Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26035)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在统一量子力学与引力理论，解决二者在基础层面的不兼容问题，并为量子引力提供一个基于量子信息和纠缠的全新视角。

Method: 定义基于纠缠图的信息论距离，构建尺度依赖的分形时空；通过非可微轨迹导出随机测地线和复协变导数；从时间依赖的纠缠诱导度规推导引力动力学。

Result: 成功导出薛定谔方程作为涌现动力学规律，得到爱因斯坦引力宏观极限及含分形修正项的广义场方程；预测维度约化、修正引力势及短程量子行为偏离。

Conclusion: FEQG框架为量子理论与引力提供了统一的信息起源，表明二者皆源于量子纠缠结构，具有可观测的分形引力与量子修正效应。

Abstract: In this paper we propose that spacetime is an emergent fractal geometry generated by the entanglement structure of an underlying quantum information network. Indeed, it is developed a framework in which spacetime, quantum mechanics, and gravity emerge from the entanglement structure of a universal quantum state. Geometry is defined by an information-theoretic distance $d_{ij}=-\ell_0\log(I_{ij}/I_0)$ on an entanglement graph, producing a scale-dependent, fractal spacetime whose effective dimension flows toward $D\to 2$ near the Planck scale. In this fractal geometry, nondifferentiable trajectories lead to stochastic geodesics and a complex covariant derivative, from which the Schrödinger equation follows as an emergent dynamical law. Gravity arises from the time dependence of the entanglement-induced metric, yielding Einstein gravity in the macroscopic limit and fractal corrections encoded in a generalized field equation $G_{μν}=8πG(T_{μν}+αE_{μν}+βF_{μν})$. The resulting \emph{Fractal Entanglement Quantum Gravity} (FEQG) framework predicts dimensional reduction, modified gravitational potentials, and possible deviations from standard quantum mechanics at ultrashort scales, offering a unified informational origin for quantum theory and gravitation.

</details>
